81	
82	def _is_placeholder_z(z: float) -> bool:
83	    return math.isfinite(_f(z)) and abs(float(z) - PLACEHOLDER_Z) < 1.0e-6
84	
85	
86	# Yamasaki & Totani 2020 ApJ 888,105 give a Galactic hot-halo DM ~43 pc/cm^3
87	# along typical sightlines; 40 is a round, deliberately conservative prior.
88	DM_MW_HALO = 40.0
89	
90	# Macquart+2020 Nature 581,391 / Deng & Zhang 2014: f_IGM ~ 0.84 of cosmic
91	# baryons are in the diffuse ionized IGM, with electron fraction chi_e ~ 7/8
92	# (H + singly-ionized He).
93	F_IGM = 0.84
94	CHI_E = 0.875
95	
96	
97	def _f(value: Any) -> float:
98	    """Coerce to float, returning NaN for missing/non-finite/sentinel values."""
99	    if value is None:
100	        return math.nan
101	    try:
102	        if np.ma.is_masked(value):
103	            return math.nan
104	    except TypeError:
105	        return math.nan
106	    try:
107	        out = float(value)
108	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
109	        return math.nan
110	    if not math.isfinite(out) or out <= -9990.0:
111	        return math.nan
112	    return out
113	
114	
115	def _truthy(value: Any) -> bool:
116	    if isinstance(value, str):
117	        return value.strip().lower() in {"true", "t", "1", "yes", "y"}
118	    try:
119	        if pd.isna(value):
120	            return False
121	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
122	        pass
123	    return bool(value)
124	
125	
126	def dm_cosmic_macquart(z: float, f_igm: float = F_IGM, chi_e: float = CHI_E) -> float:
127	    """Return the Macquart-relation mean cosmic/IGM DM at redshift z (pc/cm^3)."""
128	    z_value = float(z)
129	    if not math.isfinite(z_value) or z_value <= 0.0:
130	        return 0.0
131	
132	    from scipy.integrate import quad
133	
134	    # Deng & Zhang 2014 ApJ 783,L35 / Macquart+2020 Nature 581,391:
135	    # <DM_cosmic> = n_e,0 * (c/H0) * int_0^z (1+z')/E(z') dz', with the present
136	    # diffuse-IGM electron density n_e,0 = f_IGM chi_e Omega_b rho_crit,0 / m_p.
137	    n_e0 = (f_igm * chi_e * config.COSMO.Ob0 * config.COSMO.critical_density0 / const.m_p).to(
138	        u.cm**-3
139	    )
140	    hubble_dist = (const.c / config.COSMO.H0).to(u.pc)
141	    integral, _ = quad(
142	        lambda zp: (1.0 + zp) / math.sqrt(config.COSMO.Om0 * (1.0 + zp) ** 3 + config.COSMO.Ode0),
143	        0.0,
144	        z_value,
145	    )
146	    dm = (n_e0 * hubble_dist * integral).to(u.pc / u.cm**3).value
147	    return float(dm)
148	
149	
150	def parse_dm_obs(path_or_name: str | None) -> float | None:
151	    """Extract the observed DM from a burst filename/id.
152	
153	    The codetection bursts encode DM as the integer token after the Stokes flag,
154	    e.g. ``casey_chime_I_491_2085_32000b_cntr_bpc.npy`` -> 491 (DSA paths used a
155	    lowercase 'l' where Stokes 'I' was intended).
156	    """
157	    if not path_or_name:
158	        return None
159	    import re
160	
161	    stem = os.path.basename(str(path_or_name))
162	    m = re.search(r"_[IlL]_(\d+)_\d+_\d+b", stem)
163	    if not m:
164	        return None
165	    try:
166	        return float(m.group(1))
167	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
168	        return None
169	
170	
171	def read_measured_tau_ms(fit_json_path: str | None) -> float | None:
172	    """Return tau_1ghz (ms) from a scat_analysis fit_results.json, else None.
173	
174	    This is a raw parser: it returns the best-fit tau regardless of fit quality.
175	    Use read_tau_fit for the quality-gated value with uncertainties.
176	    """
177	    if not fit_json_path or not os.path.exists(fit_json_path):
178	        return None
179	    try:
180	        with open(fit_json_path) as fh:
181	            data = json.load(fh)
182	    except (OSError, ValueError):
183	        return None
184	    params = data.get("best_params", {}) if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
185	    tau = params.get("tau_1ghz")
186	    tau_f = _f(tau)
187	    if not math.isfinite(tau_f) or tau_f <= 0.0:
188	        return None
189	    return float(tau_f)
190	
191	
192	def read_tau_fit(fit_json_path: str | None) -> dict | None:
193	    """Read tau_1ghz with uncertainty and quality flag from a fit_results.json.
194	
195	    Prefers the posterior median + 16/84 from best_params_percentiles (the
196	    uncertainty-bearing summary); falls back to the best_params point estimate.
197	    Returns {tau, err_minus, err_plus, quality_flag, chi2_reduced} or None when no
198	    usable tau is present. The quality_flag is the pipeline's recalibrated
199	    chi2-driven gate (PASS/MARGINAL/FAIL); callers decide whether to ingest.
200	    """
201	    if not fit_json_path or not os.path.exists(fit_json_path):
202	        return None
203	    try:
204	        with open(fit_json_path) as fh:
205	            data = json.load(fh)
206	    except (OSError, ValueError):
207	        return None
208	    if not isinstance(data, dict):
209	        return None
210	
211	    err_minus = err_plus = math.nan
212	    tau_f = math.nan
213	    pct = data.get("best_params_percentiles")
214	    if isinstance(pct, dict) and isinstance(pct.get("tau_1ghz"), dict):
215	        tau_pct = pct["tau_1ghz"]
216	        tau_f = _f(tau_pct.get("median"))
217	        err_minus = _f(tau_pct.get("err_minus"))
218	        err_plus = _f(tau_pct.get("err_plus"))
219	    if not math.isfinite(tau_f):
220	        params = data.get("best_params", {}) or {}
221	        tau_f = _f(params.get("tau_1ghz"))
222	    if not math.isfinite(tau_f) or tau_f <= 0.0:
223	        return None
224	
225	    gof = data.get("goodness_of_fit", {}) or {}
226	    return {
227	        "tau": float(tau_f),
228	        "err_minus": err_minus,
229	        "err_plus": err_plus,
230	        "quality_flag": gof.get("quality_flag"),
231	        "chi2_reduced": _f(gof.get("chi2_reduced")),
232	    }
233	
234	
235	_PYGEDM: Any = None
236	
237	
238	def _load_pygedm() -> Any:
239	    """Import pygedm once, shimming the SciPy>=1.14 removal of integrate.simps."""
240	    global _PYGEDM
241	    if _PYGEDM is not None:
242	        return _PYGEDM
243	    try:
244	        import scipy.integrate as _si
245	
246	        if not hasattr(_si, "simps"):
247	            # pygedm.yt2020 still calls the removed scipy.integrate.simps; the
248	            # successor simpson made x keyword-only, so wrap rather than alias.
249	            _si.simps = lambda y, x=None, *a, **k: _si.simpson(y, x=x, *a, **k)
250	        import warnings
251	
252	        with warnings.catch_warnings():
253	            warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
254	            import pygedm
255	        _PYGEDM = pygedm
256	    except Exception:
257	        _PYGEDM = False
258	    return _PYGEDM
259	
260	
261	def galactic_dm_tau(l_deg: float, b_deg: float, method: str = "ne2001", dist_pc: float = 30000.0):
262	    """Return (DM_NE2001, DM_YMW16, tau_MW_ms) toward Galactic (l, b).
263	
264	    Uses the compiled pygedm NE2001/YMW16 models (offline). Returns NaNs if
265	    pygedm is unavailable so callers degrade gracefully.
266	    """
267	    pg = _load_pygedm()
268	    if not pg:
269	        return (math.nan, math.nan, math.nan)
270	    import warnings
271	
272	    with warnings.catch_warnings():
273	        warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
274	        dm_ne, tau_ne = pg.dist_to_dm(l_deg, b_deg, dist_pc, method="ne2001")
275	        dm_yw, tau_yw = pg.dist_to_dm(l_deg, b_deg, dist_pc, method="ymw16")
276	    tau = tau_ne if method == "ne2001" else tau_yw
277	    try:
278	        tau_ms = float(tau.to(u.ms).value)
279	    except (AttributeError, u.UnitConversionError):
280	        tau_ms = float(tau) * 1.0e3
281	    return (float(dm_ne.value), float(dm_yw.value), tau_ms)
282	
283	
284	def _find_burst_config_path(name: str, configs_dir: str) -> str | None:
285	    """Return the data path string from a target's CHIME burst config yaml."""
286	    if not configs_dir or not os.path.isdir(configs_dir):
287	        return None
288	    target = name.lower()
289	    for fn in os.listdir(configs_dir):
290	        if not fn.lower().endswith((".yaml", ".yml")):
291	            continue
292	        if not fn.lower().startswith(target):
293	            continue
294	        try:
295	            with open(os.path.join(configs_dir, fn)) as fh:
296	                for line in fh:
297	                    if line.strip().startswith("path:"):
298	                        return line.split("path:", 1)[1].strip()
299	        except OSError:
300	            continue
301	    return None
302	
303	
304	def _lookup_dm_obs(name: str, configs_dir: str | None) -> float | None:
305	    if configs_dir is None:
306	        configs_dir = os.path.join("scattering", "configs", "bursts", "chime")
307	    return parse_dm_obs(_find_burst_config_path(name, configs_dir))
308	
309	
310	def _lookup_tau_fit(name: str, bursts_dir: str | None) -> dict | None:
311	    """Find the best CHIME scattering fit for a target across result locations.
312	
313	    Returns the read_tau_fit dict, preferring a quality-PASS fit when several are
314	    present (so a re-run that supersedes an old FAIL is picked up). Returns None
315	    if no fit_results.json with a usable tau exists.
316	    """
317	    target = name.lower()
318	    candidates: list[str] = []
319	    search_dirs = []
320	    if bursts_dir:
321	        search_dirs.append(os.path.join(bursts_dir, target))
322	        search_dirs.append(bursts_dir)
323	    search_dirs.append(os.path.join("scattering", "scat_process"))
324	    seen = set()
325	    for d in search_dirs:
326	        for pat in (f"{target}*chime*fit_results.json", f"{target}*fit_results.json"):
327	            for path in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(d, pat))):
328	                if path not in seen:
329	                    seen.add(path)
330	                    candidates.append(path)
331	
332	    best = None
333	    for path in candidates:
334	        fit = read_tau_fit(path)
335	        if fit is None:
336	            continue
337	        if fit.get("quality_flag") == "PASS":
338	            return fit
339	        if best is None:
340	            best = fit
341	    return best
342	
343	
344	def _scattering_verdict(tau_obs: float, tau_interv: float, tau_interv_hi: float, n_fg: int) -> str:
345	    if not math.isfinite(tau_obs) or tau_obs <= 0.0:
346	        return f"no scattering measurement (predicted intervening tau={tau_interv:.2g} ms)"
347	    if n_fg == 0:
348	        return "no intervening screen; scattering is host / Milky-Way"
349	    ratio = tau_interv / tau_obs
350	    ratio_hi = (tau_interv_hi / tau_obs) if math.isfinite(tau_interv_hi) else ratio
351	    if ratio >= 0.5:
352	        return f"intervening galaxy plausibly dominates (pred/obs={ratio:.2f})"
353	    if ratio_hi >= 0.5:
354	        return f"intervening galaxy may contribute (prior-upper pred/obs={ratio_hi:.2f})"
355	    if ratio >= 0.05:
356	        return f"intervening subdominant; host / Milky-Way dominates (pred/obs={ratio:.2g})"
357	    return f"intervening negligible; host / Milky-Way dominated (pred/obs={ratio:.2g})"
358	
359	
360	def _dm_verdict(dm_obs, dm_mw_ism, dm_mw_halo, dm_cosmic, dm_interv, dm_host) -> str:
361	    if not math.isfinite(dm_obs):
362	        return "no observed DM"
363	    if dm_host < -0.05 * max(dm_obs, 1.0):
364	        return "over-budget (MW+cosmic mean exceed observed; sightline below cosmic mean)"
365	    comps = {
366	        "Milky-Way": _nz(dm_mw_ism) + dm_mw_halo,
367	        "cosmic/IGM": dm_cosmic,
368	        "intervening galaxy": dm_interv,
369	        "host": max(dm_host, 0.0),
370	    }
371	    dominant = max(comps, key=lambda k: comps[k])
372	    return f"{dominant}-dominated"
373	
374	
375	def _nz(x: float) -> float:
376	    """Treat NaN as 0 for additive budget closure (model-unavailable component)."""
377	    return x if math.isfinite(x) else 0.0
378	
379	
380	def build_sightline_budget(
381	    name: str,
382	    ra_str: str,
383	    dec_str: str,
384	    z_frb: float,
385	    *,
386	    results_dir: str = "results",
387	    configs_dir: str | None = None,
388	    bursts_dir: str | None = None,
389	    enrich: bool = False,
390	    dm_mw_fn: Callable | None = None,
391	    dm_obs: float | None = None,
392	    tau_obs: float | None = None,
393	    dm_mw_halo: float = DM_MW_HALO,
394	) -> dict:
395	    """Assemble the full DM + scattering budget for one FRB sightline."""
396	    sight = SkyCoord(ra_str, dec_str, unit=(u.hourangle, u.deg))
397	    gal = sight.galactic
398	    l_deg, b_deg = float(gal.l.deg), float(gal.b.deg)
399	
400	    if dm_mw_fn is None:
401	        dm_mw_fn = galactic_dm_tau
402	    dm_mw_ism, dm_mw_ism_ymw16, tau_mw_ms = dm_mw_fn(l_deg, b_deg, "ne2001")
403	
404	    if dm_obs is None:
405	        dm_obs = _lookup_dm_obs(name, configs_dir)
406	
407	    # Scattering measurement: gate ingestion on the recalibrated quality flag.
408	    # An injected tau_obs (tests / manual override) is trusted as-is; otherwise a
409	    # fit_results.json is ingested ONLY when quality_flag == "PASS". A present but
410	    # non-PASS fit is recorded (so the verdict can say "fit present but not
411	    # locked in") but its tau is withheld from the budget.
412	    tau_obs_err_minus = tau_obs_err_plus = math.nan
413	    tau_obs_chi2 = math.nan
414	    if tau_obs is not None:
415	        tau_obs_quality = "INJECTED"
416	    else:
417	        tau_obs_quality = None
418	        fit = _lookup_tau_fit(name, bursts_dir)
419	        if fit is not None:
420	            tau_obs_quality = fit.get("quality_flag") or "UNKNOWN"
421	            tau_obs_chi2 = _f(fit.get("chi2_reduced"))
422	            if fit.get("quality_flag") == "PASS":
423	                tau_obs = fit.get("tau")
424	                tau_obs_err_minus = _f(fit.get("err_minus"))
425	                tau_obs_err_plus = _f(fit.get("err_plus"))
426	            # non-PASS: tau_obs stays None (withheld), quality recorded above
427	
428	    dm_hot = dm_cool = 0.0
429	    dm_hot_cap = dm_cool_cap = 0.0
430	    min_b_over_rvir = math.inf
431	    tau_int = tau_int_lo = tau_int_hi = 0.0
432	    n_fg = n_isect = 0
433	    dom: dict[str, Any] = {
434	        "best_z": math.nan,
435	        "best_b_over_rvir": math.nan,
436	        "best_g_scatt": math.nan,
437	        "best_cool_fc": math.nan,
438	        "best_logM": math.nan,
439	        "best_mass_source": None,
440	        "best_impact_kpc": math.nan,
441	    }
442	    best_tau = -1.0
443	
444	    csv_path = os.path.join(results_dir, f"{name.lower()}_galaxies.csv")
445	    if os.path.exists(csv_path):
446	        matches = pd.read_csv(csv_path)
447	        unified = build_unified_records(
448	            matches, z_frb=z_frb, sight_ra=sight.ra.deg, sight_dec=sight.dec.deg, enrich=enrich
449	        )
450	        for _, row in unified.iterrows():
451	            z = _f(row.get("z"))
452	            if not (math.isfinite(z) and z < float(z_frb)):  # foreground galaxies only
453	                continue
454	            n_fg += 1
455	            if _truthy(row.get("intersects_rvir")):
456	                n_isect += 1
457	            dh_raw = _nz(_f(row.get("dm_halo")))
458	            dc_raw = _nz(_f(row.get("dm_cool")))
459	            dm_hot += dh_raw
460	            dm_cool += dc_raw
461	
462	            # Capped intervening DM: re-evaluate the hot-halo column at the
463	            # CGM-regime floor max(b, 0.1 R_vir), then rescale the cool phase by
464	            # the same hot-column ratio (the cool term is sub-dominant).
465	            bor = _f(row.get("b_over_rvir"))
466	            if math.isfinite(bor):
467	                min_b_over_rvir = min(min_b_over_rvir, bor)
468	            m_halo = _f(row.get("M_halo"))
469	            rvir = _f(row.get("R_vir_kpc"))
470	            impact = _f(row.get("impact_kpc"))
471	            if (
472	                math.isfinite(bor)
473	                and bor < INTERIOR_B_OVER_RVIR
474	                and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (m_halo, rvir, impact))
475	                and rvir > 0.0
476	                and dh_raw > 0.0
477	            ):
478	                b_cap = max(impact, INTERIOR_B_OVER_RVIR * rvir)
479	                dh_cap = _nz(_f(scat.dm_halo_mnfw(m_halo, z, b_cap)))
480	                dc_cap = dc_raw * (dh_cap / dh_raw) if dh_raw > 0.0 else dc_raw
481	            else:
482	                dh_cap, dc_cap = dh_raw, dc_raw
483	            dm_hot_cap += dh_cap
484	            dm_cool_cap += dc_cap
485	
486	            t = _f(row.get("pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz"))
487	            if math.isfinite(t):
488	                tau_int += t
489	                tl = _f(row.get("pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz_lo"))
490	                th = _f(row.get("pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz_hi"))
491	                tau_int_lo += tl if math.isfinite(tl) else t
492	                tau_int_hi += th if math.isfinite(th) else t
493	                if t > best_tau:
494	                    best_tau = t
495	                    dom = {
496	                        "best_z": z,
497	                        "best_b_over_rvir": _f(row.get("b_over_rvir")),
498	                        "best_g_scatt": _f(row.get("g_scatt")),
499	                        "best_cool_fc": _f(row.get("cool_fc")),
500	                        "best_logM": _f(row.get("logM_best")),
501	                        "best_mass_source": (
502	                            str(row.get("mass_source"))
503	                            if row.get("mass_source") is not None
504	                            else None
505	                        ),
506	                        "best_impact_kpc": _f(row.get("impact_kpc")),
507	                    }
508	
509	    dm_intervening = dm_hot + dm_cool
510	    dm_intervening_capped = dm_hot_cap + dm_cool_cap
511	    if n_fg == 0:
512	        dm_regime = "none"
513	    elif math.isfinite(min_b_over_rvir) and min_b_over_rvir < INTERIOR_B_OVER_RVIR:
514	        dm_regime = "GALAXY_INTERIOR"
515	    else:
516	        dm_regime = "CGM"
517	
518	    # Without a real host redshift the cosmic/host decomposition is undefined.
519	    z_is_placeholder = _is_placeholder_z(z_frb)
520	    dm_cosmic = math.nan if z_is_placeholder else dm_cosmic_macquart(float(z_frb))
521	    dm_obs_f = _f(dm_obs)
522	    if math.isfinite(dm_obs_f) and not z_is_placeholder:
523	        base = dm_obs_f - _nz(dm_mw_ism) - dm_mw_halo - dm_cosmic
524	        dm_host = base - dm_intervening
525	        dm_host_capped = base - dm_intervening_capped
526	        dm_host_rest = dm_host * (1.0 + float(z_frb))
527	        dm_host_rest_capped = dm_host_capped * (1.0 + float(z_frb))
528	    else:
529	        dm_host = dm_host_capped = dm_host_rest = dm_host_rest_capped = math.nan
530	
531	    # Reliability of the intervening DM/tau: which mass the dominant screen used.
532	    intervening_mass_source = dom.get("best_mass_source")
533	    if intervening_mass_source is None:
534	        intervening_mass_confidence = "none"
535	    elif intervening_mass_source in _MEASURED_MASS_SOURCES:
536	        intervening_mass_confidence = "measured"
537	    else:
538	        intervening_mass_confidence = "assumed"
539	
540	    tau_obs_f = _f(tau_obs)
541	    if not math.isfinite(tau_obs_f) and tau_obs_quality not in (None, "PASS", "INJECTED"):
542	        # A fit exists but failed the quality gate -> its tau is withheld.
543	        verdict_scat = (
544	            f"scattering fit present but quality_flag={tau_obs_quality} "
545	            f"(chi2_red={tau_obs_chi2:.1f}); tau not locked in"
546	            if math.isfinite(tau_obs_chi2)
547	            else f"scattering fit present but quality_flag={tau_obs_quality}; tau not locked in"
548	        )
549	    else:
550	        verdict_scat = _scattering_verdict(tau_obs_f, tau_int, tau_int_hi, n_fg)
551	    if z_is_placeholder:
552	        verdict_dm = "z_frb is a placeholder (unknown host z); cosmic & host DM budget unavailable"
553	    else:
554	        # The capped intervening DM is the physically bounded one; base the
555	        # dominant-component verdict on it, and note when the raw core-
556	        # extrapolated value over-shoots the observed budget.
557	        verdict_dm = _dm_verdict(
558	            dm_obs_f, dm_mw_ism, dm_mw_halo, dm_cosmic, dm_intervening_capped, dm_host_capped
559	        )
560	        if (
561	            dm_regime == "GALAXY_INTERIOR"
562	            and math.isfinite(dm_host)
563	            and dm_host < -0.05 * max(dm_obs_f, 1.0)
564	        ):
565	            verdict_dm += (
566	                "; raw intervening DM is core-extrapolated and exceeds the observed budget"
567	            )
568	
569	    flags = {
570	        "z_frb": "PLACEHOLDER" if z_is_placeholder else "MEASURED",
571	        "dm_obs": "MEASURED" if math.isfinite(dm_obs_f) else "NOT_AVAILABLE",
572	        "dm_mw_ism": "MODEL" if math.isfinite(_f(dm_mw_ism)) else "NOT_AVAILABLE",
573	        "dm_mw_halo": "PRIOR",
574	        "dm_cosmic": "NOT_AVAILABLE (placeholder z)" if z_is_placeholder else "PREDICTED_MEAN",
575	        "dm_intervening": f"PREDICTED ({intervening_mass_confidence} mass)",
576	        "dm_intervening_capped": f"PREDICTED ({dm_regime})",
577	        "dm_host": "RESIDUAL" if math.isfinite(dm_host) else "NOT_AVAILABLE",
578	        "tau_obs": (
579	            f"MEASURED ({tau_obs_quality})"
580	            if math.isfinite(tau_obs_f)
581	            else (
582	                f"WITHHELD ({tau_obs_quality})"
583	                if tau_obs_quality not in (None, "PASS", "INJECTED")
584	                else "NOT_AVAILABLE"
585	            )
586	        ),
587	        "tau_mw": "MODEL" if math.isfinite(_f(tau_mw_ms)) else "NOT_AVAILABLE",
588	        "tau_intervening": "PREDICTED",
589	    }
590	
591	    record = {
592	        "name": name,
593	        "z_frb": float(z_frb),
594	        "l_deg": l_deg,
595	        "b_deg": b_deg,
596	        "dm_obs": dm_obs_f,
597	        "dm_mw_ism": _f(dm_mw_ism),
598	        "dm_mw_ism_ymw16": _f(dm_mw_ism_ymw16),
599	        "dm_mw_halo": float(dm_mw_halo),
600	        "dm_cosmic": dm_cosmic,
601	        "dm_intervening": dm_intervening,
602	        "dm_intervening_hot": dm_hot,
603	        "dm_intervening_cool": dm_cool,
604	        "dm_intervening_capped": dm_intervening_capped,
605	        "dm_intervening_hot_capped": dm_hot_cap,
606	        "dm_intervening_cool_capped": dm_cool_cap,
607	        "dm_intervening_regime": dm_regime,
608	        "intervening_mass_source": intervening_mass_source,
609	        "intervening_mass_confidence": intervening_mass_confidence,
610	        "z_is_placeholder": z_is_placeholder,
611	        "min_b_over_rvir": min_b_over_rvir if math.isfinite(min_b_over_rvir) else math.nan,
612	        "dm_host": dm_host,
613	        "dm_host_capped": dm_host_capped,
614	        "dm_host_rest": dm_host_rest,
615	        "dm_host_rest_capped": dm_host_rest_capped,
616	        "tau_obs_ms": tau_obs_f if math.isfinite(tau_obs_f) else math.nan,
617	        "tau_obs_err_minus": tau_obs_err_minus,
618	        "tau_obs_err_plus": tau_obs_err_plus,
619	        "tau_obs_quality": tau_obs_quality,
620	        "tau_obs_chi2_reduced": tau_obs_chi2,
621	        "tau_mw_ms": _f(tau_mw_ms),
622	        "tau_intervening_ms": tau_int,
623	        "tau_intervening_lo": tau_int_lo,
624	        "tau_intervening_hi": tau_int_hi,
625	        "n_foreground": n_fg,
626	        "n_intersecting": n_isect,
627	        "verdict_scattering": verdict_scat,
628	        "verdict_dm": verdict_dm,
629	        "cgm_budget_flags": flags,
630	    }
631	    record.update(dom)
632	    return record
633	
634	
635	def build_all_budgets(
636	    targets=None,
637	    results_dir: str = "results",
638	    configs_dir: str | None = None,
639	    bursts_dir: str | None = None,
640	    enrich: bool = False,
641	    dm_mw_fn: Callable | None = None,
642	    dm_obs_map: Mapping[str, float] | None = None,
643	    tau_obs_map: Mapping[str, float] | None = None,
644	) -> pd.DataFrame:
645	    """Build the DM + scattering budget for every configured sightline."""
646	    if targets is None:
647	        targets = config.TARGETS
648	    rows = []
649	    for name, ra_str, dec_str, z_frb in targets:
650	        dm_obs = dm_obs_map.get(name) if dm_obs_map is not None else None
651	        tau_obs = tau_obs_map.get(name) if tau_obs_map is not None else None
652	        rows.append(
653	            build_sightline_budget(
654	                name,
655	                ra_str,
656	                dec_str,
657	                z_frb,
658	                results_dir=results_dir,
659	                configs_dir=configs_dir,
660	                bursts_dir=bursts_dir,
661	                enrich=enrich,
662	                dm_mw_fn=dm_mw_fn,
663	                dm_obs=dm_obs,
664	                tau_obs=tau_obs,
665	            )
666	        )
667	    return pd.DataFrame(rows)
668	
669	
670	def format_budget_table(df: pd.DataFrame) -> str:
671	    """Render the DM + scattering budget as a GitHub-flavored markdown table."""
672	    headers = [
673	        "Sightline",
674	        "z",
675	        "DM_obs",
676	        "DM_MW",
677	        "DM_cosmic",
678	        "DM_interv_raw",
679	        "DM_interv_cap",
680	        "regime",
681	        "interv_mass",
682	        "DM_host_cap",
683	        "tau_obs(ms)",
684	        "tau_interv(ms)",
685	        "scattering attribution",
686	    ]
687	    lines = ["| " + " | ".join(headers) + " |", "|" + "|".join(["---"] * len(headers)) + "|"]
688	
689	    def fmt(x, spec):
690	        v = _f(x)
691	        return format(v, spec) if math.isfinite(v) else "-"
692	
693	    for _, r in df.iterrows():
694	        cells = [
695	            str(r["name"]),
696	            fmt(r.get("z_frb"), ".3f"),
697	            fmt(r.get("dm_obs"), ".0f"),
698	            fmt(_nz(_f(r.get("dm_mw_ism"))) + _f(r.get("dm_mw_halo")), ".0f"),
699	            fmt(r.get("dm_cosmic"), ".0f"),
700	            fmt(r.get("dm_intervening"), ".1f"),
701	            fmt(r.get("dm_intervening_capped"), ".1f"),
702	            str(r.get("dm_intervening_regime", "")),
703	            str(r.get("intervening_mass_confidence", "")),
704	            fmt(r.get("dm_host_capped"), ".0f"),
705	            fmt(r.get("tau_obs_ms"), ".3g"),
706	            fmt(r.get("tau_intervening_ms"), ".2g"),
707	            str(r.get("verdict_scattering", "")),
708	        ]
709	        lines.append("| " + " | ".join(cells) + " |")
710	    return "\n".join(lines)
711	
712	
713	# Styling consistent with the rest of the module.
714	DARK_BLUE = "#1B365D"
715	MW_COLOR = "#4A90E2"
716	HALO_COLOR = "#7FB3E8"
717	COSMIC_COLOR = "#9B59B6"
718	INTERV_COLOR = "#F5A623"
719	HOST_COLOR = "#D0021B"
720	TEXT_DARK = "#333333"
721	GRID_COLOR = "#E5E5E5"
722	BG_LIGHT = "#FAFBFC"
723	
724	
725	def make_budget_figure(df: pd.DataFrame):
726	    """Two-panel figure: stacked DM budget + predicted-vs-measured scattering."""
727	    d = df.copy()
728	    names = [str(n) for n in d["name"]]
729	    y = np.arange(len(d))[::-1]
730	
731	    fig, (ax_dm, ax_tau) = plt.subplots(
732	        1,
733	        2,
734	        figsize=(14, max(3.5, 0.55 * len(d) + 1.6)),
735	        dpi=150,
736	        facecolor=BG_LIGHT,
737	        gridspec_kw={"width_ratios": [1.7, 1.0]},
738	    )
739	    for ax in (ax_dm, ax_tau):
740	        ax.set_facecolor(BG_LIGHT)
741	
742	    def col(name):
743	        return (
744	            pd.to_numeric(d.get(name, pd.Series(np.nan, index=d.index)), errors="coerce")
745	            .fillna(0.0)
746	            .to_numpy(float)
747	        )
748	
749	    mw = col("dm_mw_ism")
750	    halo = col("dm_mw_halo")
751	    cosmic = col("dm_cosmic")
752	    interv = col("dm_intervening_capped")
753	    interv_raw = col("dm_intervening")
754	    host = np.maximum(col("dm_host_capped"), 0.0)
755	    regime = [str(v) for v in d.get("dm_intervening_regime", pd.Series([""] * len(d)))]
756	
757	    left = np.zeros(len(d))
758	    for vals, color, label in (
759	        (mw, MW_COLOR, "MW ISM (NE2001)"),
760	        (halo, HALO_COLOR, "MW halo"),
761	        (cosmic, COSMIC_COLOR, "cosmic/IGM <DM>"),
762	        (interv, INTERV_COLOR, "intervening CGM (b>=0.1Rvir cap)"),
763	        (host, HOST_COLOR, "host (residual)"),
764	    ):
765	        ax_dm.barh(
766	            y, vals, left=left, color=color, edgecolor="white", linewidth=0.6, label=label, zorder=3
767	        )
768	        left = left + vals
769	
770	    dm_obs = col("dm_obs")
771	    for yi, xo in zip(y, dm_obs):
772	        if xo > 0:
773	            ax_dm.plot([xo, xo], [yi - 0.4, yi + 0.4], color=TEXT_DARK, lw=2.0, zorder=5)
774	
775	    # Flag core-extrapolated sightlines and show the raw (uncapped) intervening DM.
776	    for yi, reg, raw, cap in zip(y, regime, interv_raw, interv):
777	        if reg == "GALAXY_INTERIOR" and raw > cap:
778	            ax_dm.annotate(
779	                f"raw interv DM={raw:.0f} (core-extrap.)",
780	                xy=(0, yi),
781	                xytext=(4, -2),
782	                textcoords="offset points",
783	                va="top",
784	                ha="left",
785	                fontsize=7,
786	                color=HOST_COLOR,
787	                zorder=6,
788	            )
789	
790	    # Mark sightlines whose host redshift is a placeholder (no cosmic/host budget).
791	    placeholder = [_truthy(v) for v in d.get("z_is_placeholder", pd.Series([False] * len(d)))]
792	    for yi, ph, xo in zip(y, placeholder, dm_obs):
793	        if ph:
794	            ax_dm.annotate(
795	                "z placeholder - no cosmic/host budget",
796	                xy=(max(xo, 1.0), yi),
797	                xytext=(6, 0),
798	                textcoords="offset points",
799	                va="center",
800	                ha="left",
801	                fontsize=7,
802	                color=COSMIC_COLOR,
803	                zorder=6,
804	            )
805	    ax_dm.set_yticks(y)
806	    ax_dm.set_yticklabels(names, fontsize=10)
807	    ax_dm.set_xlabel("DM (pc cm$^{-3}$)", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold", color=TEXT_DARK)
808	    ax_dm.set_title(
809	        "DM budget per sightline  (black bar = observed DM)",
810	        fontsize=12,
811	        fontweight="bold",
812	        color=DARK_BLUE,
813	        pad=10,
814	    )
815	    ax_dm.legend(
816	        loc="lower right", fontsize=8, frameon=True, facecolor="white", edgecolor=GRID_COLOR
817	    )
818	    ax_dm.grid(True, axis="x", linestyle=":", color=GRID_COLOR, alpha=0.8, zorder=0)
819	
820	    tau_obs = col("tau_obs_ms")
821	    tau_int = col("tau_intervening_ms")
822	    tau_lo = col("tau_intervening_lo")
823	    tau_hi = col("tau_intervening_hi")
824	    for yi, to, ti, tl, th in zip(y, tau_obs, tau_int, tau_lo, tau_hi):
825	        if ti > 0:
826	            ax_tau.plot(
827	                [max(tl, 1e-7), max(th, 1e-7)], [yi, yi], color=INTERV_COLOR, lw=2, zorder=2
828	            )
829	            ax_tau.scatter(
830	                [ti],
831	                [yi],
832	                color=INTERV_COLOR,
833	                s=45,
834	                zorder=3,
835	                label="predicted intervening" if yi == y[0] else None,
836	            )
837	        if to > 0:
838	            tlo = (
839	                _f(d["tau_obs_err_minus"].iloc[list(y).index(yi)])
840	                if "tau_obs_err_minus" in d.columns
841	                else math.nan
842	            )
843	            thi = (
844	                _f(d["tau_obs_err_plus"].iloc[list(y).index(yi)])
845	                if "tau_obs_err_plus" in d.columns
846	                else math.nan
847	            )
848	            if math.isfinite(tlo) and math.isfinite(thi) and (tlo > 0 or thi > 0):
849	                ax_tau.errorbar(
850	                    [to],
851	                    [yi],
852	                    xerr=[[max(tlo, 0)], [max(thi, 0)]],
853	                    fmt="none",
854	                    ecolor=TEXT_DARK,
855	                    elinewidth=1.2,
856	                    capsize=3,
857	                    zorder=4,
858	                )
859	            ax_tau.scatter(
860	                [to],
861	                [yi],
862	                marker="D",
863	                color=TEXT_DARK,
864	                s=55,
865	                zorder=5,
866	                label="measured burst (PASS)" if yi == y[0] else None,
867	            )
868	
869	    # Mark sightlines with a fit present but withheld by the quality gate.
870	    quality = [str(v) for v in d.get("tau_obs_quality", pd.Series([""] * len(d)))]
871	    withheld_labeled = False
872	    for yi, q, ti in zip(y, quality, tau_int):
873	        if q in ("FAIL", "MARGINAL", "UNKNOWN"):
874	            x_at = max(ti, 1e-6)
875	            ax_tau.scatter(
876	                [x_at],
877	                [yi],
878	                marker="x",
879	                color=HOST_COLOR,
880	                s=50,
881	                zorder=4,
882	                label="fit withheld (failed gate)" if not withheld_labeled else None,
883	            )
884	            withheld_labeled = True
885	
886	    ax_tau.set_yticks(y)
887	    ax_tau.set_yticklabels([])
888	    positive = (
889	        np.concatenate([tau_obs[tau_obs > 0], tau_int[tau_int > 0]]) if len(d) else np.array([])
890	    )
891	    if positive.size:
892	        ax_tau.set_xscale("log")
893	    ax_tau.set_xlabel(
894	        r"$\tau_{\rm scat}$ at 1 GHz (ms)", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold", color=TEXT_DARK
895	    )
896	    ax_tau.set_title(
897	        "Scattering: predicted intervening vs measured",
898	        fontsize=12,
899	        fontweight="bold",
900	        color=DARK_BLUE,
901	        pad=10,
902	    )
903	    handles, labels = ax_tau.get_legend_handles_labels()
904	    if handles:
905	        ax_tau.legend(loc="best", fontsize=8, frameon=True, facecolor="white", edgecolor=GRID_COLOR)
906	    ax_tau.grid(True, axis="x", linestyle=":", color=GRID_COLOR, alpha=0.8)
907	
908	    fig.suptitle(
909	        "FRB sightline DM & scattering budgets (priors + measurements)",
910	        fontsize=13,
911	        fontweight="bold",
912	        color=DARK_BLUE,
913	    )
914	    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0, 1, 0.97))
915	    return fig
916	
917	
918	def main():
919	    base_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
920	    results_dir = os.path.join(base_dir, "results")
921	    configs_dir = os.path.join(base_dir, "scattering", "configs", "bursts", "chime")
922	    bursts_dir = os.path.join(results_dir, "bursts")
923	
924	    df = build_all_budgets(
925	        results_dir=results_dir, configs_dir=configs_dir, bursts_dir=bursts_dir, enrich=False
926	    )
927	
928	    # Emit TNS designations (not internal nicknames) in the CSV, table, and figure (#26).
929	    from scattering.scat_analysis.burst_metadata import load_tns_name
930	
931	    df["name"] = [load_tns_name(n) for n in df["name"]]
932	
933	    csv_path = os.path.join(results_dir, "sightline_dm_scattering_budget.csv")
934	    df.to_csv(csv_path, index=False)

937	    md_path = os.path.join(results_dir, "sightline_dm_scattering_budget.md")
938	    with open(md_path, "w") as fh:

940	        fh.write(format_budget_table(df))
941	        fh.write("\n")

946	    fig.savefig(png_path, dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")

950

> AGENT

The budget sums over all foreground halos per sightline and tracks the dominant one, but the per-sightline `{name}_galaxies.csv` inputs aren't on disk. Clusters are fully reproducible from the table (R500 = b ÷ (b/R500), M500 from R500+z, DM via `dm_cluster_beta_model`). Galaxies need the dominant halo's mass. Let me check whether the search can regenerate offline or if catalogs are cached anywhere, and read the config + build_unified mass logic.

1	"""Configuration and target list for galaxy search v2.0."""
2	
3	from astropy.cosmology import Planck18
4	
5	# Cosmology to use throughout the module
6	COSMO = Planck18
7	
8	# Default search parameters
9	DEFAULT_IMPACT_KPC = 100.0
10	DEFAULT_CLUSTER_IMPACT_KPC = (
11	    5000.0  # 5 Mpc: clusters intercept sightlines far beyond galaxy-scale impacts
12	)
13	DEFAULT_Z_EPS = 0.01  # Redshift buffer for foreground search
14	MIN_Z_SEARCH = 0.005  # Minimum redshift cutoff to avoid infinite search cone
15	# Minimum credible photometric redshift: DESI Legacy photo-z (VII/292/north) pin
16	# unreliable sources near z~0 (z=0.001-0.008 floor noise). Applies only to rows
17	# carrying a photo-z error; spec-z catalogs (NED, GLADE+) are exempt so genuine
18	# nearby galaxies (e.g. UGC 06371, z=0.009) survive.
19	FOREGROUND_PHOTOZ_FLOOR = 0.01
20	# Velocity-offset window (km/s) below which a spec-z neighbour is host/local
21	# ambiguous, not a clean foreground. dv = c*(z_frb - z_gal)/(1 + z_frb); a galaxy
22	# inside this window sits at the host's recession velocity (group member / host
23	# peculiar velocity ~200-500 km/s) and cannot be cleanly attributed as an
24	# intervening system. Set at the upper edge of typical group dispersions.
25	FOREGROUND_AMBIGUITY_KMS = 500.0
26	SPEED_OF_LIGHT_KMS = 299792.458
27	# Spec-z catalogs: redshift is spectroscopic / distance-derived, so any z_err they
28	# carry (e.g. GLADE+'s generic 0.015 floor) is NOT a per-object photo-z and must
29	# not gate via the photo-z error path. Matched on the `catalog` column substring;
30	# DESI VII/292/north is the lone photo-z catalog.
31	SPEC_Z_CATALOG_SUBSTRINGS = ("ned", "vii/291", "glade", "sdss", "desi_dr1")
32	PHOTO_Z_CATALOG_SUBSTRINGS = ("vii/292",)
33	MAX_SEARCH_RADIUS_DEG = (
34	    2.0  # Cap angular query radius (low-z clusters otherwise blow up Vizier query cones)
35	)

1	"""Build unified per-galaxy CGM/scattering records from matched catalogs."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import math
6	import os
7	from typing import Any
8	
9	import astropy.units as u
10	import numpy as np
11	import pandas as pd
12	from astropy.coordinates import SkyCoord
13	
14	from galaxies.v2_0 import cgm_observables as cgm
15	from galaxies.v2_0 import config, enrichers
16	from galaxies.v2_0 import scattering_predict as scat
17	from galaxies.v2_0.generate_galaxy_plots import (
18	    estimate_halo_mass,
19	    estimate_logmstar_from_photometry,
20	    get_rvir_and_rs,
21	    nfw_enclosed_mass,
22	)
23	
24	MASS_PRIORITY = (
25	    "glade_catalog",
26	    "xsc_kband",
27	    "desi_ls_sed",
28	    "wise_w1",
29	    "ps1_taylor",
30	    "cluster_catalog",
31	    "assumed",
32	)
33	
34	
35	def _is_bad(value: Any) -> bool:
36	    if value is None:
37	        return True
38	    try:
39	        if np.ma.is_masked(value):
40	            return True
41	    except TypeError:
42	        return True
43	    try:
44	        numeric = float(value)
45	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
46	        return True
47	    return not math.isfinite(numeric) or numeric <= -9990.0
48	
49	
50	def _has(row: pd.Series, name: str) -> bool:
51	    return name in row.index
52	
53	
54	def _num(row: pd.Series, *names: str) -> float:
55	    for name in names:
56	        if not _has(row, name):
57	            continue
58	        value = row[name]
59	        if not _is_bad(value):
60	            return float(value)
61	    return math.nan
62	
63	
64	def _text(row: pd.Series, name: str) -> str | None:
65	    if not _has(row, name):
66	        return None
67	    value = row[name]
68	    if value is None:
69	        return None
70	    try:
71	        if np.ma.is_masked(value):
72	            return None
73	    except TypeError:
74	        return None
75	    try:
76	        if pd.isna(value):
77	            return None
78	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
79	        pass
80	    if isinstance(value, float) and math.isnan(value):
81	        return None
82	    text = str(value)
83	    return text if text else None
84	
85	
86	def _finite(value: Any) -> bool:
87	    return not _is_bad(value)
88	
89	
90	def _or_nan(value: Any) -> float:
91	    return math.nan if _is_bad(value) else float(value)
92	
93	
94	def _boolish(value: Any) -> bool:
95	    if value is None:
96	        return False
97	    try:
98	        if np.ma.is_masked(value):
99	            return False
100	    except TypeError:
101	        return False
102	    if isinstance(value, (bool, np.bool_)):
103	        return bool(value)
104	    if isinstance(value, str):
105	        return value.strip().lower() in {"true", "t", "1", "yes", "y"}
106	    if _is_bad(value):
107	        return False
108	    return bool(value)
109	
110	
111	def _distmod(z_gal: float) -> float:
112	    if _is_bad(z_gal) or float(z_gal) <= 0.0:
113	        return math.nan
114	    return float(config.COSMO.distmod(float(z_gal)).value)
115	
116	
117	def select_stellar_mass(row, z_gal) -> tuple[float, str, str]:
118	    """Return the best available log10 stellar mass and provenance."""
119	    catalog = (_text(row, "catalog") or "").lower()
120	    glade_mass = _num(row, "M_star")
121	    if "glade" in catalog and _finite(glade_mass):
122	        return glade_mass, "glade_catalog", "glade_catalog"
123	
124	    if _finite(z_gal) and float(z_gal) > 0.0:
125	        xsc_kmag = _num(row, "xsc_kmag")
126	        if _finite(xsc_kmag):
127	            # Bell & de Jong 2001 place near-IR M/L_K near unity for normal
128	            # galaxies; with Willmer 2018 M_sun,K=3.28 Vega this gives an
129	            # explicit order-of-magnitude 2MASS prior when no SED fit exists.
130	            abs_k = xsc_kmag - _distmod(float(z_gal))
131	            if _finite(abs_k):
132	                return -0.4 * (abs_k - 3.28), "xsc_kband", "xsc_kband"
133	
134	        desi_mass = cgm.stellar_mass_desi_gz(
135	            _num(row, "desi_ls_gmag"),
136	            _num(row, "desi_ls_zmag"),
137	            float(z_gal),
138	        )
139	        if _finite(desi_mass):
140	            return float(desi_mass), "desi_ls_sed", "desi_ls_gz_zibetti2009"
141	
142	        wise_mass = cgm.stellar_mass_wise_w1(
143	            _num(row, "W1mag"),
144	            float(z_gal),
145	            w1_w2=_num(row, "wise_W1_W2"),
146	        )
147	        if _finite(wise_mass):
148	            return float(wise_mass), "wise_w1", "wise_w1_cluver2014"
149	
150	        ps1_mass = estimate_logmstar_from_photometry(row, float(z_gal))
151	        if _finite(ps1_mass):
152	            return float(ps1_mass), "ps1_taylor", "ps1_taylor2011"
153	
154	    return 10.0, "assumed", "assumed_default"
155	
156	
157	def _empty_with_unified_columns(matches: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
158	    out = matches.copy()
159	    for column in _UNIFIED_COLUMNS:
160	        if column not in out.columns:
161	            out[column] = pd.Series(dtype=object if column in _OBJECT_COLUMNS else "float64")
162	    return out
163	
164	
165	_OBJECT_COLUMNS = {
166	    "mass_source",
167	    "mass_method",
168	    "morph_type",
169	    "is_star_forming",
170	    "wise_agn_flag",
171	    "desi_is_agn",
172	    "bpt_class",
173	    "group_member",
174	    "group_n",
175	    "intersects_rvir",
176	    "sfr_is_limit",
177	    "cgm_extractable_flags",
178	    "z_source",
179	}
180	
181	_UNIFIED_COLUMNS = (
182	    "z_source",
183	    "z_err",
184	    "logM_best",
185	    "mass_source",
186	    "mass_method",
187	    "M_halo",
188	    "logM_halo",
189	    "R_vir_kpc",
190	    "r_s",
191	    "c",
192	    "b_over_rvir",
193	    "intersects_rvir",
194	    "gi_color",
195	    "gr_color",
196	    "morph_type",
197	    "sersic",
198	    "shape_r_kpc",
199	    "axis_ratio",
200	    "position_angle_deg",
201	    "inclination_deg",
202	    "azimuthal_phi_deg",
203	    "is_star_forming",
204	    "W1mag",
205	    "W2mag",
206	    "W3mag",
207	    "W4mag",
208	    "wise_W1_W2",
209	    "wise_agn_flag",
210	    "galex_fuv",
211	    "galex_nuv",
212	    "galex_ebv",
213	    "sfr_w3",
214	    "sfr_uv",
215	    "sfr_best",
216	    "sfr_is_limit",
217	    "metallicity_12logOH",
218	    "desi_oii_flux",
219	    "desi_halpha_flux",
220	    "desi_is_agn",
221	    "bpt_class",
222	    "group_member",
223	    "group_n",
224	    "pred_mgii_wr",
225	    "cool_fc",
226	    "cool_fc_lo",
227	    "cool_fc_hi",
228	    "dm_halo",
229	    "dm_cool",
230	    "f_tilde",
231	    "f_tilde_lo",
232	    "f_tilde_hi",
233	    "g_scatt",
234	    "pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz",
235	    "pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz_lo",
236	    "pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz_hi",
237	    "pred_tau_hot_ms_1GHz",
238	    "pred_tau_cool_ms_1GHz",
239	    "pred_scint_bw_khz",
240	    "scattering_rank",
241	    "cgm_extractable_flags",
242	)
243	
244	
245	def build_unified_records(
246	    matches, z_frb, sight_ra, sight_dec, enrich=True, enrich_fn=None
247	) -> pd.DataFrame:
248	    """Build one unified per-galaxy DataFrame from foreground matches."""
249	    if matches is None:
250	        matches = pd.DataFrame()
251	    if len(matches) == 0:
252	        return _empty_with_unified_columns(matches)
253	
254	    if enrich:
255	        if enrich_fn is None:
256	            enrich_fn = enrichers.enrich_all_catalogs
257	        out = enrich_fn(matches)
258	    else:
259	        out = matches.copy()
260	    out = out.copy()
261	
262	    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
263	    for _, row in out.iterrows():
264	        z_gal = _num(row, "z")
265	        impact_kpc = _num(row, "impact_kpc")
266	        logm_best, mass_source, mass_method = select_stellar_mass(row, z_gal)
267	
268	        m_halo = logm_halo = r_vir = r_s = concentration = math.nan
269	        if _finite(logm_best):
270	            try:
271	                m_halo = float(estimate_halo_mass(logm_best))
272	                logm_halo = math.log10(m_halo)
273	                r_vir, r_s, concentration = get_rvir_and_rs(m_halo, z_gal)
274	            except (ArithmeticError, ValueError, RuntimeError, OverflowError):
275	                m_halo = logm_halo = r_vir = r_s = concentration = math.nan
276	
277	        b_over_rvir = (
278	            impact_kpc / r_vir
279	            if _finite(impact_kpc) and _finite(r_vir) and r_vir > 0.0
280	            else math.nan
281	        )
282	        intersects_rvir = bool(_finite(impact_kpc) and _finite(r_vir) and impact_kpc <= r_vir)
283	
284	        gmag = _num(row, "gmag")
285	        imag = _num(row, "imag")
286	        gi_color = gmag - imag if _finite(gmag) and _finite(imag) else math.nan
287	        desi_g = _num(row, "desi_ls_gmag")
288	        desi_r = _num(row, "desi_ls_rmag")
289	        gr_color = desi_g - desi_r if _finite(desi_g) and _finite(desi_r) else math.nan
290	
291	        shape_r_arcsec = _num(row, "desi_ls_shape_r")
292	        shape_r_kpc = math.nan
293	        if _finite(shape_r_arcsec) and _finite(z_gal) and z_gal > 0.0:
294	            shape_r_kpc = (
295	                shape_r_arcsec
296	                * config.COSMO.kpc_proper_per_arcmin(z_gal).to(u.kpc / u.arcmin).value
297	                / 60.0
298	            )
299	
300	        e1 = _num(row, "shape_e1", "desi_ls_shape_e1")
301	        e2 = _num(row, "shape_e2", "desi_ls_shape_e2")
302	        axis_ratio = math.nan
303	        position_angle = math.nan
304	        if _finite(e1) and _finite(e2):
305	            axis_ratio = _or_nan(cgm.axis_ratio_from_ellipticity(e1, e2))
306	            position_angle = _or_nan(cgm.position_angle_deg(e1, e2))
307	        if not _finite(axis_ratio):
308	            axis_ratio = _num(row, "xsc_axis_ratio")
309	        if not _finite(position_angle):
310	            position_angle = _num(row, "xsc_pa")
311	
312	        inclination = _or_nan(cgm.inclination_deg(axis_ratio)) if _finite(axis_ratio) else math.nan
313	        gal_ra = _num(row, "ra")
314	        gal_dec = _num(row, "dec")
315	        phi = (
316	            _or_nan(
317	                cgm.azimuthal_angle_phi_deg(gal_ra, gal_dec, position_angle, sight_ra, sight_dec)
318	            )
319	            if _finite(position_angle)
320	            else math.nan
321	        )
322	
323	        is_star_forming = bool(cgm.is_star_forming(gr_color, logm_best))
324	        w1mag = _num(row, "W1mag")
325	        w2mag = _num(row, "W2mag")
326	        w3mag = _num(row, "W3mag")
327	        w4mag = _num(row, "W4mag")
328	        wise_w1_w2 = _num(row, "wise_W1_W2")
329	        wise_agn_flag = bool(cgm.wise_agn_stern2012(wise_w1_w2))
330	        galex_fuv = _num(row, "galex_fuv")
331	        galex_nuv = _num(row, "galex_nuv")
332	        galex_ebv = _num(row, "galex_ebv")
333	
334	        sfr_w3 = _or_nan(cgm.sfr_wise_w3(w3mag, z_gal))
335	        sfr_uv = _or_nan(
336	            cgm.sfr_uv_nuv(galex_nuv, z_gal, ebv=galex_ebv if _finite(galex_ebv) else 0.0)
337	        )
338	        sfr_best = sfr_w3 if _finite(sfr_w3) else sfr_uv
339	        sfr_is_limit = not (_finite(w3mag) or _finite(galex_nuv))
340	        metallicity = _or_nan(cgm.metallicity_mzr(logm_best, z_gal))
341	
342	        desi_is_agn = _text(row, "desi_is_agn")
343	        if desi_is_agn is None and _has(row, "desi_is_agn"):
344	            desi_is_agn = row["desi_is_agn"]
345	        agn_for_scattering = wise_agn_flag or _boolish(desi_is_agn)
346	
347	        pred_mgii_wr = _or_nan(scat.predict_mgii_wr(impact_kpc, logm_best))
348	        cool_fc = cool_fc_lo = cool_fc_hi = math.nan
349	        try:
350	            cool_fc, cool_fc_lo, cool_fc_hi = scat.cool_covering_fraction(
351	                b_over_rvir,
352	                logm_best,
353	                is_star_forming,
354	                phi_deg=phi,
355	            )
356	        except (ArithmeticError, ValueError, RuntimeError, OverflowError):
357	            pass
358	
359	        dm_halo = _or_nan(scat.dm_halo_mnfw(m_halo, z_gal, impact_kpc))
360	        dm_cool = _or_nan(scat.dm_cool(dm_halo, cool_fc, mgii_wr=pred_mgii_wr))
361	        f_tilde, f_tilde_lo, f_tilde_hi = scat.f_tilde_prior(
362	            sfr_best,
363	            metallicity_12logOH=metallicity,
364	            agn=agn_for_scattering,
365	        )
366	        g_scatt = scat.g_scatt(z_gal, z_frb)
367	        # g_scatt == 0 means the intervening-screen geometry has no leverage:
368	        # the galaxy is at/behind the FRB (z_gal >= z_frb) or its redshift is
369	        # invalid. In that case the screen model cannot predict burst scattering,
370	        # so tau is reported as "not predictable" (NaN) rather than a literal 0
371	        # ("no scattering"). Foreground galaxies (g_scatt > 0) keep a finite
372	        # prediction even when the stellar mass is an assumed default.
373	        screen_predictable = _finite(g_scatt) and float(g_scatt) > 0.0
374	        if screen_predictable:
375	            # Two-phase intervening screen: smooth hot halo (dm_halo) plus the
376	            # clumpy cool CGM (dm_cool, fed by covering fraction + MgII), the
377	            # latter carrying a boosted turbulence parameter. Total tau is the
378	            # sum; lo/hi additionally bracket the F_cool/F_hot prior.
379	            f_cool = f_tilde * scat.COOL_CLUMP_BOOST if _finite(f_tilde) else f_tilde
380	            tau_hot = _or_nan(scat.tau_scat_ms(f_tilde, g_scatt, dm_halo, z_gal, nu_ghz=1.0))
381	            tau_cool = _or_nan(scat.tau_scat_ms(f_cool, g_scatt, dm_cool, z_gal, nu_ghz=1.0))
382	            tau = _or_nan(
383	                scat.tau_scat_two_phase(f_tilde, g_scatt, dm_halo, dm_cool, z_gal, nu_ghz=1.0)
384	            )
385	            tau_lo = _or_nan(
386	                scat.tau_scat_two_phase(
387	                    f_tilde_lo,
388	                    g_scatt,
389	                    dm_halo,
390	                    dm_cool,
391	                    z_gal,
392	                    nu_ghz=1.0,
393	                    cool_clump_boost=scat.COOL_CLUMP_BOOST_LO,
394	                )
395	            )
396	            tau_hi = _or_nan(
397	                scat.tau_scat_two_phase(
398	                    f_tilde_hi,
399	                    g_scatt,
400	                    dm_halo,
401	                    dm_cool,
402	                    z_gal,
403	                    nu_ghz=1.0,
404	                    cool_clump_boost=scat.COOL_CLUMP_BOOST_HI,
405	                )
406	            )
407	            scint_bw = _or_nan(scat.scint_bandwidth_khz(tau))
408	        else:
409	            tau = tau_hot = tau_cool = tau_lo = tau_hi = scint_bw = math.nan
410	
411	        desi_emission_measured = _has(row, "desi_emission_matched") and _boolish(
412	            row["desi_emission_matched"]
413	        )
414	        flags = {
415	            "stellar_mass": "MEASURED" if mass_source in set(MASS_PRIORITY[:-1]) else "PREDICTED",
416	            "morphology": "MEASURED" if _finite(axis_ratio) else "NOT_AVAILABLE",
417	            "sfr": "MEASURED" if (_finite(w3mag) or _finite(galex_nuv)) else "NOT_AVAILABLE",
418	            "wise": "MEASURED" if _finite(w1mag) else "NOT_AVAILABLE",
419	            "desi_spectro": "MEASURED" if desi_emission_measured else "NOT_AVAILABLE",
420	            "dm_halo": "PREDICTED",
421	            "dm_cool": "PREDICTED",
422	            "tau_scat": "PREDICTED" if screen_predictable else "NOT_PREDICTABLE",
423	            "cool_covering": "PREDICTED",
424	        }
425	
426	        # Catalog cluster (m500_msun present): override the galaxy derivation with
427	        # a catalog-M200 halo and a beta-model ICM DM. Clusters contribute DM but
428	        # negligible scattering (Connor 2023; Lee 2023), so cool-CGM / tau are
429	        # zeroed and the stellar-mass ladder is bypassed.
430	        m500_msun = _num(row, "m500_msun")
431	        if _finite(m500_msun) and m500_msun > 0.0:
432	            m_halo = config.CLUSTER_M500_TO_M200 * m500_msun
433	            logm_best = logm_halo = math.log10(m_halo)
434	            mass_source, mass_method = "cluster_catalog", "cluster_catalog_m500"
435	            r_vir = scat.r_delta_kpc(m_halo, z_gal, 200)
436	            r_s = concentration = math.nan
437	            b_over_rvir = (
438	                impact_kpc / r_vir
439	                if _finite(impact_kpc) and _finite(r_vir) and r_vir > 0.0
440	                else math.nan
441	            )
442	            intersects_rvir = bool(_finite(impact_kpc) and _finite(r_vir) and impact_kpc <= r_vir)
443	            r500_kpc = _num(row, "r500_kpc")
444	            dm_halo = _or_nan(
445	                scat.dm_cluster_beta_model(
446	                    m500_msun,
447	                    z_gal,
448	                    impact_kpc,
449	                    r500_kpc=(r500_kpc if _finite(r500_kpc) else None),
450	                )
451	            )
452	            dm_cool = 0.0
453	            pred_mgii_wr = math.nan
454	            cool_fc = cool_fc_lo = cool_fc_hi = 0.0
455	            f_tilde = f_tilde_lo = f_tilde_hi = math.nan
456	            tau = tau_lo = tau_hi = tau_hot = tau_cool = 0.0
457	            scint_bw = math.nan
458	            flags = {
459	                "stellar_mass": "MEASURED",  # catalog M500
460	                "morphology": "NOT_AVAILABLE",
461	                "sfr": "NOT_AVAILABLE",
462	                "wise": "NOT_AVAILABLE",
463	                "desi_spectro": "NOT_AVAILABLE",
464	                "dm_halo": "PREDICTED",
465	                "dm_cool": "NOT_APPLICABLE",
466	                "tau_scat": "NOT_APPLICABLE",
467	                "cool_covering": "NOT_APPLICABLE",
468	            }
469	
470	        records.append(
471	            {
472	                "z_source": _text(row, "catalog"),
473	                "z_err": _num(row, "z_best_err", "e_zphot", "z_phot_err"),
474	                "logM_best": logm_best,
475	                "mass_source": mass_source,
476	                "mass_method": mass_method,
477	                "M_halo": m_halo,
478	                "logM_halo": logm_halo,
479	                "R_vir_kpc": r_vir,
480	                "r_s": r_s,
481	                "c": concentration,
482	                "b_over_rvir": b_over_rvir,
483	                "intersects_rvir": intersects_rvir,
484	                "gi_color": gi_color,
485	                "gr_color": gr_color,
486	                "morph_type": _text(row, "desi_ls_type") or _text(row, "type"),
487	                "sersic": _num(row, "desi_ls_sersic"),
488	                "shape_r_kpc": shape_r_kpc,
489	                "axis_ratio": axis_ratio,
490	                "position_angle_deg": position_angle,
491	                "inclination_deg": inclination,
492	                "azimuthal_phi_deg": phi,
493	                "is_star_forming": is_star_forming,
494	                "W1mag": w1mag,
495	                "W2mag": w2mag,
496	                "W3mag": w3mag,
497	                "W4mag": w4mag,
498	                "wise_W1_W2": wise_w1_w2,
499	                "wise_agn_flag": wise_agn_flag,
500	                "galex_fuv": galex_fuv,
501	                "galex_nuv": galex_nuv,
502	                "galex_ebv": galex_ebv,
503	                "sfr_w3": sfr_w3,
504	                "sfr_uv": sfr_uv,
505	                "sfr_best": sfr_best,
506	                "sfr_is_limit": sfr_is_limit,
507	                "metallicity_12logOH": metallicity,
508	                "desi_oii_flux": _num(row, "desi_oii_flux"),
509	                "desi_halpha_flux": _num(row, "desi_halpha_flux"),
510	                "desi_is_agn": desi_is_agn,
511	                "bpt_class": None,
512	                "group_member": False,
513	                "group_n": 0,
514	                "pred_mgii_wr": pred_mgii_wr,
515	                "cool_fc": cool_fc,
516	                "cool_fc_lo": cool_fc_lo,
517	                "cool_fc_hi": cool_fc_hi,
518	                "dm_halo": dm_halo,
519	                "dm_cool": dm_cool,
520	                "f_tilde": f_tilde,
521	                "f_tilde_lo": f_tilde_lo,
522	                "f_tilde_hi": f_tilde_hi,
523	                "g_scatt": g_scatt,
524	                "pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz": tau,
525	                "pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz_lo": tau_lo,
526	                "pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz_hi": tau_hi,
527	                "pred_tau_hot_ms_1GHz": tau_hot,
528	                "pred_tau_cool_ms_1GHz": tau_cool,
529	                "pred_scint_bw_khz": scint_bw,
530	                "cgm_extractable_flags": flags,
531	            }
532	        )
533	
534	    unified = out.reset_index(drop=True).copy()
535	    derived = pd.DataFrame(records)
536	    for column in derived.columns:
537	        unified[column] = derived[column]
538	    unified["scattering_rank"] = _scattering_ranks(unified["pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz"])
539	    return unified
540	
541	
542	def _scattering_ranks(values: pd.Series) -> list[int]:
543	    numeric = pd.to_numeric(values, errors="coerce")
544	    order = sorted(
545	        range(len(numeric)),
546	        key=lambda i: (
547	            not np.isfinite(numeric.iloc[i]),
548	            -numeric.iloc[i] if np.isfinite(numeric.iloc[i]) else 0.0,
549	            i,
550	        ),
551	    )
552	    ranks = [0] * len(numeric)
553	    for rank, idx in enumerate(order, start=1):
554	        ranks[idx] = rank
555	    return ranks
556	
557	
558	def build_for_target(
559	    name, ra_str, dec_str, z_frb, results_dir="results", enrich=True
560	) -> pd.DataFrame:
561	    """Read a target match CSV, write its lower-case unified CSV, and return it."""
562	    base = name.lower()
563	    input_path = os.path.join(results_dir, f"{base}_galaxies.csv")
564	    if not os.path.exists(input_path):
565	        print(f"Warning: missing input CSV {input_path}")
566	        return pd.DataFrame()
567	
568	    matches = pd.read_csv(input_path)
569	    sight = SkyCoord(ra_str, dec_str, unit=(u.hourangle, u.deg))
570	    unified = build_unified_records(
571	        matches,
572	        z_frb=z_frb,
573	        sight_ra=sight.ra.deg,
574	        sight_dec=sight.dec.deg,
575	        enrich=enrich,
576	    )
577	    unified.to_csv(os.path.join(results_dir, f"{base}_unified.csv"), index=False)
578	    return unified
579	
580	
581	def build_all(results_dir="results", enrich=True) -> dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
582	    """Build unified records for every configured target."""
583	    return {
584	        name: build_for_target(name, ra_str, dec_str, z_frb, results_dir=results_dir, enrich=enrich)
585	        for name, ra_str, dec_str, z_frb in config.TARGETS
586	    }
587	
588	
589	__all__ = [
590	    "MASS_PRIORITY",
591	    "build_all",
592	    "build_for_target",
593	    "build_unified_records",
594	    "nfw_enclosed_mass",
595	    "select_stellar_mass",
596	]
597

 sc = SkyCoord(ra, dec, unit=(u.hourangle, u.deg))\n    uni = build_unified_records(df, z_frb=z_frb, sight_ra=sc.ra.deg, sight_dec=sc.dec.deg, enrich=False)\n    # foreground only, exclude clusters max dm_halo\n    dom = fg.sort_values([\"pt\", \"dh\"], ascending=False).iloc[0]\n    # sum DM_int over all foreground halos (matches budget's dm_intervening)\n    dm_hot = pd.to_numeric(fg[\"dm_halo\"], errors=\"coerce\").fillna(0).sum()\n    dm_cool = pd.to_numeric(fg[\"dm_cool\"], errors=\"coerce\").fillna(0).sum()\n    out[\"galaxies\"][name] = {\n        \"z_frb\": z_frb,\n        \"n_foreground\": int(len(fg)),\n        \"dom_z\": float(dom[\"z\"]),\n        \"dom_impact_kpc\": float(dom[\"impact_kpc\"]),\n        \"dom_M_halo\": float(dom[\"M_halo\"]),\n        \"dom_logM_halo\": float(dom[\"logM_halo\"]),\n        \"dom_R_vir_kpc\": float(dom[\"R_vir_kpc\"]),\n        \"dom_b_over_rvir\": float(dom[\"b_over_rvir\"]),\n        \"dom_mass_source\": \"sum_dm_hot\": float(dm_hot),\n        \"sum_dm_cool\": float(dm_cool),\n        \"sum_dm_int\": float(dm_hot + dm_cool),\n    }\n\n# 4 grand clusters (FRB 20230307A field) from foreground_table.tex\n# (objid, b_kpc, b_over_r500, z)\nCLUS = [\n    (\"J115120.4+714435\", 604.0, 0.83, 0.200),\n    (\"J115128.2+713637\", 1055.0, 1.25, 0.192),\n    (\"J114944.0+714348\", 1569.0, 2.96, 0.244),\n    (\"J115140.5+712732\", 2105.0, 3.32, 0.176),\n]\nRHO_FACTOR = 4.0 / 3.0 * math.pi * 500.0\nfor objid, b_kpc, b_over_r500, z in CLUS:\n    r500 = b_kpc / b_over_r500\n    rho_crit = COSMO.critical_density(z).to(u.Msun / u.kpc**3).value\n    m500 = RHO_FACTOR * rho_crit * r500**3\n    dm_at_b = scat.dm_cluster_beta_model(m500, z, b_kpc, r500_kpc=r500)\n    dm_at_core = scat.dm_cluster_beta_model(m500, z, 0.0, r500_kpc=r500)\n    out[\"clusters\"][objid] = {\n        \"b_kpc\": b_kpc, \"b_over_r500\": b_over_r500, \"z\": z,\n        \"r500_kpc\": r500, \"m500_msun\": m500, \"logM500\": math.log10(m500),\n        \"dm_icm_at_b\": dm_at_b, \"dm_icm_at_core\": dm_at_core,\n        \"inside_r500\": b_over_r500 < 1.0,\n    }\n\nprint(json.dumps(out, indent=2))\n"

All 8 systems verified, reproducing the photoz-fix budget exactly (Phineas ΣDM_int=733.1, Whitney=363.6, Casey=235.8, Isha=112.5). Dominant halos: Phineas/Whitney/Casey are interior (b/R_vir<0.1), Isha is a CGM grazer (0.14). Clusters: only J115120.4 is inside R500 (β-model ceiling ~180 pc/cm³); the outer two are beyond truncation (DM=0).

\"whitney\": 363.6,\n    \"casey\": 235.8,\n    \"isha\": 112.5,\n}\n\n_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nDEFAULT_GAL_DIR = os.path.join(_REPO, \"scratch\", \"photoz-fix\")\nDEFAULT_OUT_DIR = \"/Users/jakobfaber/Developer/overleaf/Faber2026/figures\"\n\n\ndef dominant_foreground_halo(name: str, ra: str, dec: str, z_frb: float, gal_dir: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Return the dominant foreground halo record + sightline DM sum for a galaxy.\"\"\"\n    df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(gal_dir, f\"{name}_galaxies.csv\"))\n    sc = SkyCoord(ra, dec, unit=(u.hourangle, u.deg))\n    uni = build_unified_records(df, z_frb=z_frb, sight_ra=sc.ra.deg, sight_dec=sc.dec.deg, enrich=False)\n    z = pd.to_numeric(uni[\"z\"], errors=\"coerce\")\n    fg = uni[(z < z_frb) & (uni[\"mass_source\"] != \"cluster_catalog\")].copy()\n    fg[\"_pt\"] = pd.to_numeric(fg[\"pred_tau_scat_ms_1GHz\"], errors=\"coerce\").fillna(-1.0)\n    fg[\"_dh\"] = errors=\"coerce\").fillna(0.0).sum()\n    sum_cool = pd.to_numeric(fg[\"dm_cool\"], errors=\"coerce\").fillna(0.0).sum()\n    return {\n        \"z_gal\": float(dom[\"z\"]),\n        \"impact_kpc\": int(len(fg)),\n        \"sum_dm_int\": float(sum_hot + sum_cool),\n    }\n\n\ndef cluster_params(b_kpc: float, b_over_r500: float, z: float) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Recover R500, M500 and the beta-model rho_crit * r500**3\n    return {\n        \"r500_kpc\": r500,\n        \"m500_msun\": m500,\n        \"logM500\": math.log10(m500),\n        \"dm_at_b\": scat.dm_cluster_beta_model(m500, z, b_kpc, r500_kpc=r500),\n    }\n\n\ndef _mass_label(source: str) -> str:\n    return {\n        \"glade_catalog\": \"GLADE+ $M_\\\\star$\",\n        \"xsc_kband\": \"2MASS $K$ $M_\\\\star$\",\n        \"desi_ls_sed\": \"DESI SED $M_\\\\star$\",\n        \"wise_w1\": \"WISE $W1$ $M_\\\\star$\",\n        \"ps1_taylor\": \"PS1 $M_\\\\star$\",\n        \"assumed\": \"assumed $L_\\\\star$\",\n    }.get(source, source)\n\n\ndef make_galaxy_figure(gal_dir: str):\n    \"\"\"2x2 mNFW hot-halo DM(b) panels for the four grand galaxies.\"\"\"\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(9.6, 7.6), dpi=150, facecolor=BG_LIGHT)\n    for ax, (name, tns, ra, dec, z_frb) in zip(axes.ravel(), GAL_TARGETS):\n        ax.set_facecolor(BG_LIGHT)\n        d = dominant_foreground_halo(name, ra, dec, z_frb, gal_dir)\n        rvir, b, mh, zg = d[\"r_vir\"], d[\"impact_kpc\"], d[\"m_halo\"], d[\"z_gal\"]\n        b_cap = INTERIOR_B_OVER_RVIR * rvir\n        interior = d[\"b_over_rvir\"] < INTERIOR_B_OVER_RVIR\n\n        # mNFW projected hot-halo DM as a function of impact parameter.\n        bb = np.linspace(0.5, rvir, 240)\n        dm_b = np.array([scat.dm_halo_mnfw(mh, zg, float(x)) or 0.0 for x in bb])\n        ax.plot(bb, dm_b, color=HALO_COLOR, lw=2.2, zorder=4, label=\"hot mNFW column\")\n\n        # Interior region (b < 0.1 R_vir): the smooth column is extrapolated and\n        # capped at the 0.1 R_vir floor.\n        ax.axvspan(0, b_cap, color=HOST_COLOR, alpha=0.07, zorder=0)\n        ax.axvline(b_cap, color=HOST_COLOR, ls=\":\", lw=1.4, zorder=3,\n                   label=\"$0.1\\\\,R_{\\\\rm vir}$ cap\")\n        ax.axvline(rvir, color=TEXT_DARK, ls=\"--\", lw=1.0, alpha=0.6, zorder=3)\n        ax.text(rvir, ax.get_ylim()[1] * 0.02, \"$R_{\\\\rm vir}$\", fontsize=7,\n                color=TEXT_DARK, ha=\"right\", va=\"bottom\", rotation=90)\n\n        # The sightline's actual impact and the DM it samples.\n        dm_at_b = scat.dm_halo_mnfw(mh, zg, b) or 0.0\n        ax.axvline(b, color=INTERV_COLOR, lw=2.0, zorder=5)\n        ax.scatter([b], [dm_at_b], color=INTERV_COLOR, s=42, zorder=6, edgecolor=\"white\")\n        ax.annotate(f\"$b={b:.1f}$ kpc\", xy=(b, dm_at_b), xytext=(8, 6),\n                    textcoords=\"offset points\", fontsize=8, color=INTERV_COLOR,\n                    fontweight=\"bold\")\n\n        regime = \"interior\" if interior else \"CGM grazer\"\n        info = (\n            f\"$z={zg:.3f}$,  $\\\\log M_{{\\\\rm halo}}={d['logM_halo']:.1f}$\\n\"\n            f\"$b/R_{{\\\\rm vir}}={d['b_over_rvir']:.3f}$ ({regime})\\n\"\n            f\"$\\\\mathrm{{DM_{{int}}}}={d['sum_dm_int']:.0f}$ \"\n            f\"(cap {scat.dm_halo_mnfw(mh, zg, max(b, b_cap)):.0f})\\n\"\n            f\"$\\\\tau_{{1\\\\rm GHz}}={d['tau']:.2g}$ ms,  {_mass_label(d['mass_source'])}\"\n        )\n        ax.text(0.97, 0.96, info, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=7.6, va=\"top\",\n                ha=\"right\", color=TEXT_DARK,\n                bbox=dict(boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.35\", fc=\"white\", ec=GRID_COLOR, alpha=0.9))\n\n        ax.set_title(f\"{tns}  ({name})\", fontsize=10, fontweight=\"bold\", color=DARK_BLUE)\n        ax.set_xlabel(\"impact parameter $b$ (kpc)\", fontsize=9, color=TEXT_DARK)\n        ax.set_ylabel(\"hot-halo DM (pc cm$^{-3}$)\", fontsize=9, color=TEXT_DARK)\n        ax.set_xlim(0, rvir)\n        ax.set_ylim(bottom=0)\n        ax.grid(True, ls=\":\", color=GRID_COLOR, alpha=0.8, zorder=0)\n        if ax is axes.ravel()[0]:\n            ax.legend(loc=\"center right\", fontsize=7.5, frameon=True, facecolor=\"white\",\n                      edgecolor=GRID_COLOR)\n\n    fig.suptitle(\n        \"Dominant intervening galaxies: mNFW circumgalactic dispersion vs. sightline impact\",\n        fontsize=12, fontweight=\"bold\", color=DARK_BLUE,\n    )\n    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0, 1, 0.96))\n    return fig\n\n\ndef make_cluster_figure():\n    \"\"\"2x2 isothermal beta-model ICM DM(b) panels for the four grand clusters.\"\"\"\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(9.6, 7.6), dpi=150, facecolor=BG_LIGHT)\n    for ax, (objid, b_kpc, b_over_r500, z) in zip(axes.ravel(), CLUSTER_TARGETS):\n        ax.set_facecolor(BG_LIGHT)\n        c = cluster_params(b_kpc, b_over_r500, z)\n        r500, m500 = c[\"r500_kpc\"], c[\"m500_msun\"]\n        r_trunc = scat.CLUSTER_R200_OVER_R500 * r500\n\n        bb = np.linspace(1.0, r_trunc, 240)\n        dm_b = np.array([scat.dm_cluster_beta_model(m500, z, float(x), r500_kpc=r500) for x in bb])\n        ax.plot(bb, dm_b, color=HALO_COLOR, lw=2.2, zorder=4, label=\"ICM $\\\\beta$-model (ceiling)\")\n\n        ax.axvspan(0, r500, color=INTERV_COLOR, alpha=0.07, zorder=0)\n        ax.axvline(r500, color=TEXT_DARK, ls=\"--\", lw=1.1, zorder=3)\n        ax.text(r500, ax.get_ylim()[1] * 0.96 if dm_b.max() > 0 else 1.0, \"$R_{500}$\",\n                fontsize=7, color=TEXT_DARK, ha=\"right\", va=\"top\", rotation=90)\n\n        inside = b_over_r500 < 1.0\n        impact_color = HOST_COLOR if inside else INTERV_COLOR\n        if b_kpc <= r_trunc:\n            ax.axvline(b_kpc, color=impact_color, lw=2.0, zorder=5)\n            ax.scatter([b_kpc], [c[\"dm_at_b\"]], color=impact_color, s=42, zorder=6,\n                       edgecolor=\"white\")\n        else:\n            # Sightline passes beyond the model truncation: mark at the right edge.\n            ax.annotate(f\"$b={b_kpc:.0f}$ kpc\\n(beyond $1.48\\\\,R_{{500}}$)\",\n                        xy=(r_trunc, 0), xytext=(-6, 14), textcoords=\"offset points\",\n                        fontsize=7.5, color=INTERV_COLOR, ha=\"right\", va=\"bottom\",\n                        fontweight=\"bold\")\n\n        verdict = \"inside $R_{500}$ (pierces ICM)\" if inside else \"outside $R_{500}$\"\n        info = (\n            f\"$z={z:.3f}$,  $\\\\log M_{{500}}={c['logM500']:.2f}$\\n\"\n            f\"$b={b_kpc:.0f}$ kpc,  $b/R_{{500}}={b_over_r500:.2f}$\\n\"\n            f\"{verdict}\\n\"\n            f\"$\\\\mathrm{{DM_{{ICM}}}}(b)\\\\lesssim {c['dm_at_b']:.0f}$ pc cm$^{{-3}}$\"\n        )\n        ax.text(0.97, 0.96, info, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=7.6, va=\"top\",\n                ha=\"right\", color=TEXT_DARK,\n                bbox=dict(boxstyle=\"round,pad=0.35\", fc=\"white\", ec=GRID_COLOR, alpha=0.9))\n\n        ax.set_title(objid, fontsize=10, fontweight=\"bold\", color=DARK_BLUE)\n        ax.set_xlabel(\"impact parameter $b$ (kpc)\", fontsize=9, color=TEXT_DARK)\n        ax.set_ylabel(\"ICM DM ceiling (pc cm$^{-3}$)\", fontsize=9, color=TEXT_DARK)\n        ax.set_xlim(0, r_trunc)\n        ax.set_ylim(bottom=0)\n        ax.grid(True, ls=\":\", color=GRID_COLOR, alpha=0.8, zorder=0)\n        if ax is axes.ravel()[0]:\n            ax.legend(loc=\"upper left\", fontsize=7.5, frameon=True, facecolor=\"white\",\n                      edgecolor=GRID_COLOR)\n\n    fig.suptitle(\n        \"Foreground clusters of FRB 20230307A: $\\\\beta$-model ICM dispersion ceiling vs. impact\",\n        fontsize=12, fontweight=\"bold\", color=DARK_BLUE,\n    )\n    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0, 1, 0.96))\n    return fig\n\n\ndef _selfcheck(gal_dir: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Assert the figure inputs reproduce the photo-z-corrected sightline budget.\"\"\"\n    for name, _tns, ra, dec, z_frb in GAL_TARGETS:\n        d = dominant_foreground_halo(name, ra, dec, z_frb, gal_dir)\n        ref, tol = _BUDGET_CHECK[name], 1.0\n        assert abs(d[\"sum_dm_int\"] - ref) < tol, f\"{name}: DM_int {d['sum_dm_int']:.1f} != {ref}\"\n        # The dominant column sampled at its own impact must match its tabulated dm_halo.\n        dm_at_b = scat.dm_halo_mnfw(d[\"m_halo\"], d[\"z_gal\"], d[\"impact_kpc\"])\n        assert abs(dm_at_b - d[\"dm_halo\"]) < 0.5, f\"{name}: dm_halo(b) mismatch\"\n    # The single R500-piercing cluster must source a non-trivial ICM ceiling; the\n    # two beyond truncation must return zero.\n    inner = cluster_params(*CLUSTER_TARGETS[0][1:])\n    assert inner[\"dm_at_b\"] > 50.0, \"inner cluster ICM ceiling unexpectedly small\"\n    for objid, b_kpc, b_over_r500, z in CLUSTER_TARGETS[2:]:\n        assert cluster_params(b_kpc, b_over_r500, z)[\"dm_at_b\"] == 0.0, f\"{objid} should be truncated\"\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gal-dir\", default=DEFAULT_GAL_DIR, help=\"dir of *_galaxies.csv inputs\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--out-dir\", default=DEFAULT_OUT_DIR, help=\"figure output directory\")\n    args = p.parse_args()\n\n    _selfcheck(args.gal_dir)\n    os.makedirs(args.out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    for fig, stem in (\n        (make_galaxy_figure(args.gal_dir), \"grand_galaxies_cgm\"),\n        (make_cluster_figure(), \"grand_clusters_icm\"),\n    ):\n        for ext in (\"pdf\", \"svg\", \"png\"):\n            path = os.path.join(args.out_dir, f\"{stem}.{ext}\")\n            fig.savefig(path, bbox_inches=\"tight\")\n            print(f\"wrote {path}\")\n        plt.close(fig)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

> AGENT

Both figures are scientifically sound and reproduce the budget. The galaxy panels show the three interior sightlines piercing deep in the core (b≪0.1 R_vir) plus Isha as a CGM grazer; the cluster panels show only J115120.4 inside R500 (~180 pc/cm³ ceiling), the rest passing outside. 

Before writing prose, I need to size the blast radius of adopting photoz-fix — which manuscript text names the now-changed systems (Mahi→Casey interior, Zach/Isha DM changes).

=== results.tex lines 4-60 (budget prose) ===
\subsection{The per-sightline dispersion and scattering budget}
\label{sec:results-budget}

Applying the decomposition of Section~\ref{sec:dm} (Eq.~\ref{eq:dmbudget}) to the
twelve sightlines yields the budget of Table~\ref{tab:budget} and
Figure~\ref{fig:budget}. The Galactic term is modest and similar across the
sample ($\mathrm{DM_{MW}}=78$--$171\,\mathrm{pc\,cm^{-3}}$), while the
cosmological mean grows with redshift to dominate the higher-$z$ sightlines. An
intervening circumgalactic column is significant only where a sightline passes
through a foreground galaxy's interior: three sightlines (FRB 20220310F, FRB 20230307A,
FRB 20230814B) fall in this galaxy-interior regime, where $\mathrm{DM_{int}}$ is
capped at $b=0.1\,R_{\mathrm{vir}}$ (the uncapped core-extrapolated columns reach
$364$--$712\,\mathrm{pc\,cm^{-3}}$), while most sightlines do not intersect a
confirmed foreground system and carry no intervening term. The host contribution
is the residual after the modeled terms are removed; for four sightlines (FRB 20220207C,
FRB 20220310F, FRB 20221203A, FRB 20230307A) it is formally negative---the modeled terms
over-predict $\mathrm{DM_{obs}}$. This is concentrated in the galaxy-interior
sightlines, where the capped circumgalactic column is most uncertain, and a
prior-predictive sensitivity analysis quantifies the probability of a negative
host term per sightline. Three sightlines (FRB 20230325A, FRB 20240122A, FRB 20230814B) lack a
spectroscopic host redshift and are carried only through the
redshift-independent Galactic term.

The predicted intervening scattering spans
$\tau_{\mathrm{int}}\sim10^{-5}$--$1.4\,\mathrm{ms}$ at $1\,\mathrm{GHz}$, largest
for the galaxy-interior sightlines (Figure~\ref{fig:budget}, right). A direct
measured-versus-predicted comparison draws on the uniform multi-component
re-fit of Section~\ref{sec:results-alpha}: its eight well-constrained sightlines
now yield a measured $(\tau_{1\,\mathrm{GHz}},\alpha)$ (Table~\ref{tab:alpha}),
while the marginal sightlines are withheld as unconstrained. We show the
predicted intervening contribution here (Figure~\ref{fig:budget}); overlaying the
measured $\tau_{1\,\mathrm{GHz}}$ on it is the principal remaining visualization
step for the sample.

\input{budget_table.tex}

\begin{figure*}
  \centering
  \includegraphics[width=0.95\textwidth]{sightline_dm_scattering_budget}
  \caption{Per-sightline dispersion and scattering budget for the twelve
  co-detections. \emph{Left:} the observed dispersion measure (black bar) of each
  burst decomposed into the Milky-Way ISM (NE2001) and halo, the cosmological mean
  (Macquart relation), the capped intervening circumgalactic column
  ($b<0.1\,R_{\mathrm{vir}}$), and the host residual; the placeholder-redshift
  sightlines (FRB 20230325A, FRB 20240122A, FRB 20230814B) show only the redshift-independent Galactic
  term. \emph{Right:} the predicted intervening pulse-broadening at $1\,\mathrm{GHz}$
  per sightline; the per-sightline measured $\tau$ from the uniform re-fit
  (Table~\ref{tab:alpha}, Section~\ref{sec:results-alpha}) are not yet overlaid on
  this figure, so only the predicted contribution is shown here.
  Generated by the \texttt{dsa110-FLITS} pipeline
  (\texttt{galaxies/v2\_0/sightline\_budget.py}).}
  \label{fig:budget}
\end{figure*}

\subsection{Scattering index and burst multiplicity}
\label{sec:results-alpha}
